# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-16 through 2026-10-22  
**Updated:** 2026-10-16T10:47:47+00:00

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-18 — Smorgasburg Diwali: Festival of Bites at Prospect Park + al di la Trattoria

**Smorgasburg Diwali: Festival of Bites at Prospect Park, then a meal.** Northern Italian at al di la Trattoria. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=248+5th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://smorgasburg.com/ny-events) · [Restaurant](https://www.aldilatrattoria.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — Smorgasburg Diwali: Festival of Bites at Williamsburg + HAGS

**Smorgasburg Diwali: Festival of Bites at Williamsburg, then a meal.** Queer fine dining and wine bar at HAGS. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=90+Kent+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=163+1st+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://smorgasburg.com/ny-events) · [Restaurant](https://www.hagsnyc.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — 30th Annual Gowanus Open Studios + Runner Up

**30th Annual Gowanus Open Studios, then a meal.** Wine bar and American small plates at Runner Up. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Various+locations+in+Gowanus%2C+Brooklyn&destination=367+7th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.artsgowanus.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.runnerupbk.com/)

### 2026-10-16 — Somssi + (Un)Silent Film: The General

**Dinner, then (Un)Silent Film: The General.** Korean at Somssi. It is a 5-minute walk from the venue. (Un)Silent Film: The General starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=22+W+8th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10011&destination=63+5th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://event.newschool.edu/unsilentfilmthegeneral) · [Restaurant](https://www.somssinyc.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — Smith Street Neighbors + Makers Fair + al di la Trattoria

**Smith Street Neighbors + Makers Fair, then a meal.** Northern Italian at al di la Trattoria. It is a 16-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Smith+St+between+Bergen+St+and+Union+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=248+5th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://mossbklyn.com/upcoming-events) · [Restaurant](https://www.aldilatrattoria.com/)

### 2026-10-16 — Funny Pages: an evening of humorous readings + Dhamaka

**Funny Pages: an evening of humorous readings, then dinner.** Indian at Dhamaka. It is a 5-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=180+Orchard+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&destination=119+Delancey+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://ptknitwear.com/events/51615) · [Restaurant](https://www.dhamaka.nyc/)

**Show Brain free concert: Big Girl, Desert Sharks, Pop Music Fever Dream, Talon, Firstsex, then a meal.** Iranian at Eyval. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.

**36th Tompkins Square Halloween Dog Parade, then a meal.** Queer fine dining and wine bar at HAGS. It is a 4-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.

## What to do this week

### 2026-10-16

#### [Open House New York Weekend Festival](https://ohny.org/festival)

10:00 AM · unusual · Many sites free; some ticketed or sold out · Various NYC locations · Manhattan · Score 75.0  
OHNY Weekend opens hundreds of notable buildings, infrastructure sites, studios, and civic spaces across the five boroughs, including many free drop-in locations.
**Timing:** Festival runs October 16-18; times vary by site.
**Practical note:** Check each site listing; Friday rain may affect outdoor queues and transit between locations.
**Why now:** The 24th annual festival runs this weekend only.
**Book soon:** Limited-access reservations sell out, but many drop-in sites remain free.
[Official](https://ohny.org/festival) · [Tickets / booking](https://ohny.org/festival) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/)

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.0  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.
[Official](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public source evidence dated through 2026-10-16; kept current restaurant openings, specific deals, prix-fixe/special menus, and dated one-day events while rejecting duplicate or stale/conflicting offers.

Dining research: 2026-10-16T10:37:12+00:00

No current Michelin star or Michelin Guide selection is verified for any candidate restaurant here. Source evidence only supports prior-affiliation facts: Eater says One Bryant Park/Kumiko Room/Kappo Sono involve chefs whose earlier restaurants held Michelin stars, and Naked Tomato chef Eyal Shani leads the Michelin-starred Shmoné; those are not awards for the new venues themselves.

### One Bryant Park and Kumiko Room open inside the former Aureole space

New openings — One Bryant Park / Kumiko Room

Large new Japanese destination with a flagship restaurant and a 14-seat omakase counter; useful for a splurge, sake/wine-heavy Midtown option. Eater reports both One Bryant Park and Kumiko Room opened on October 15, with Kappo Sono to follow in mid-November.

Price: a la carte and tasting menu; exact prices unknown  
Schedule: opened October 15; Kappo Sono planned mid-November; hours unknown  
Conditions: Eater supports the opening date and prior-Michelin-affiliation framing only: the chefs previously led or worked at Michelin-starred restaurants; no Michelin award is verified for One Bryant Park, Kumiko Room, or Kappo Sono. Kappo Sono is planned for mid-November. Source: https://ny.eater.com/restaurant-openings/414196/nyc-new-restaurant-openings-october-2026  
From: 2026-10-15  
Opened: 2026-10-15  
Published: 2026-10-14  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/restaurant-openings/414196/nyc-new-restaurant-openings-october-2026)

Schedule: debuted October 15; residency length described as months-long, exact end unknown  
Conditions: Look for the jaguar on the black door; reservation needs and service charges not verified.
